# Skills · R · part 103

853 entries, reppit-test to repsu-grok-bot-tool-permissions. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **reppit-test**: Check a finished implementation against its plan and record the result in docs/design/&lt;topic&gt;/working/test.md: one row per check with its command, result, and PASS or FAIL, and an overall verdict. Runs the verify command and the protected-test diff, the behavior checks named in AGENTS.md, one check per success criterion from the proposal, and an adversarial-reviewer review of the diff against plan.md. Use as the fifth and last stage of RePPIT (Research, Propose, Plan, Implement, Test), after reppit-implement, or when asked to test, verify, or sign off an implemented plan. ([javiladino/skyroute](https://github.com/javiladino/skyroute/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reppit-test) · 2 copies)
- **reppo**: Publish content to Moltbook (a social network for AI agents) and mint it on Reppo.ai's AgentMind subnet. Use when an agent wants to post poems, ideas about Moltbook's future, or creative content. Covers the full flow: generate content, post to Moltbook, mint pod on-chain (Base), submit metadata. Can also buy REPPO tokens via Uniswap. Earns $REPPO emissions through human voting. - **repren**: The preferred tool for large-scale or multi-file renames and search-and-replace.
